};

function propertyMapsRowHtml(names){

  const pool = (names && names.length) ? names : state.properties.filter(p=>p.active).map(p=>p.name);

  const withMaps = pool.filter(n=>PROPERTY_MAPS[n]);

  if(withMaps.length===0) return "";

  return `<div class="map-row">${withMaps.map(n=>`<button type="button" class="btn-ghost btn-small" data-map="${escAttr(n)}">🗺 ${esc(n)}</button>`).join("")}</div>`;

}

function propertyMapsCardHtml(){

  const row = propertyMapsRowHtml(null);

  if(!row) return "";

  return `<div class="card"><h2>Property maps</h2><p class="muted">Tap a property to see its stand layout.</p>${row}</div>`;

}

function showMap(name){

  const mapSrc = PROPERTY_MAPS[name];

  if(!mapSrc) return;

  const ov = document.createElement("div");

  ov.className = "map-overlay";

  ov.innerHTML = `<div class="map-title">${esc(name)}</div><img src="${mapSrc}" alt="Stand map of ${escAttr(name)}"><div style="color:#E7E1CA;margin-top:10px;font-size:13px">Tap anywhere to close</div>`;

  ov.addEventListener("click", ()=>ov.remove());

  document.body.appendChild(ov);

}

document.addEventListener("click", (e)=>{

  const mb = e.target.closest("[data-map]");

  if(mb){ e.preventDefault(); showMap(mb.dataset.map); }

});


// ---------- init / seeding ----------

async function init(){

  const core = await sbootstrap();


  if(core === null){

    // Genuine failure to reach the backend. Do NOT seed/write any defaults —

    // that would overwrite real data on the sheet. Show a retry state instead.

    state.loaded = false;

    state.loadFailed = true;

    render();

    return;

  }

  state.loadFailed = false;


  let roster = core["roster"];

  if(!roster){ roster = SEED_ROSTER.slice(); await sset("roster", roster); }

  else if(roster.length && typeof roster[0] === "string"){

    // migrate old plain-name format

    roster = roster.map(n => ({name:n, junior: JUNIOR_NAMES.has(n)}));

    await sset("roster", roster);

  }

  state.roster = roster;


  let props = core["properties"];

  if(!props){ props = SEED_PROPERTIES.map(p=>({...p, active:true})); await sset("properties", props); }

  else if(props.length && props[0].active === undefined){

    props = props.map(p=>({...p, active:true}));

    await sset("properties", props);

  }

  state.properties = props;


  let species = core["species"];

  if(!species){ species = SEED_SPECIES.slice(); await sset("species", species); }

  state.species = species;


  let idx = core["session-index"];

  if(!idx){ idx = []; await sset("session-index", idx); }

  state.sessionIndex = idx;


  let pin = core["admin-pin"];

  if(!pin){ pin = "2026"; await sset("admin-pin", pin); }

  state.adminPin = pin;


  // Sessions came back in the same bootstrap response — no separate request needed.

  state.sessionIndex.forEach(id=>{

    const s = core["session:"+id];

    if(s) state.sessions[id] = s;

  });

  // Safety net: catches anything the bootstrap response somehow missed.

  await loadAllSessions();

  state.loaded = true;

  render();

}


async function loadAllSessions(){

  const missing = state.sessionIndex.filter(id => !state.sessions[id]);

  if(missing.length === 0) return;

  const results = await sgetBatch(missing.map(id => "session:"+id));

  missing.forEach(id => {

    const s = results["session:"+id];

    if(s) state.sessions[id] = s;

  });

}

async function saveSession(s){

  state.sessions[s.id] = s;

  await sset("session:"+s.id, s);

}


function sessionsSorted(){

  return state.sessionIndex.map(id=>state.sessions[id]).filter(Boolean)

    .sort((a,b)=> a.dateISO===b.dateISO ? a.day.localeCompare(b.day) : a.dateISO.localeCompare(b.dateISO));

}

function sessionStatus(s){

  if(s.locked) return "locked";

  if(s.order && s.order.length) return "drawn";

  if(!s.active) return "closed";

  if(isPastCloseTime(s) && !s.forceOpen) return "auto-closed";

  return "open";

}


// ---------- bag count helpers ----------

function bagTotal(counts){

  if(!counts) return 0;

  return Object.values(counts).reduce((a,b)=>a+(Number(b)||0), 0);

}

function bagBreakdown(counts){

  if(!counts) return "";

  return Object.entries(counts).filter(([,n])=>Number(n)>0).map(([sp,n])=>`${sp} ${n}`).join(" · ");

}


// ---------- tabs ----------

document.addEventListener("click", (e)=>{

  const tabBtn = e.target.closest("[data-tab]");

  if(tabBtn){ state.tab = tabBtn.dataset.tab; render(); }

});


function render(){

  document.querySelectorAll(".tabbar button").forEach(b=>{

    b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.tab === state.tab);

  });

  const titles = {register:"Register to Shoot", draw:"Live Draw", rollcall:"Roll Call", results:"Draw Results", dashboard:"Season Stats", setup:"Club Setup"};

  document.getElementById("topTitle").textContent = titles[state.tab] || "Field Register";


  if(!state.loaded){

    if(state.loadFailed){

      document.getElementById("view").innerHTML = `<div class="card">

        <div class="empty">⚠️ Couldn't reach the shared database. Nothing was changed or lost — this is just a failed connection.<br><br>Check your internet connection, then try again.</div>

        <div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn-primary" id="retryLoad" style="width:100%">Retry</button></div>

      </div>`;

      const retryBtn = document.getElementById("retryLoad");

      if(retryBtn) retryBtn.addEventListener("click", ()=>{

        document.getElementById("view").innerHTML = `<div class="loading">Loading register…</div>`;

        init();

      });

    }

    return;

  }

  const view = document.getElementById("view");

  if(state.tab === "register") view.innerHTML = renderRegister();

  else if(state.tab === "draw") view.innerHTML = state.adminUnlocked ? renderDraw() : renderPinGate("draw");

  else if(state.tab === "rollcall") view.innerHTML = state.adminUnlocked ? renderRollCall() : renderPinGate("rollcall");

  else if(state.tab === "results") view.innerHTML = renderResults();

  else if(state.tab === "dashboard") view.innerHTML = renderDashboard();

  else if(state.tab === "setup") view.innerHTML = state.adminUnlocked ? renderSetup() : renderPinGate("setup");

  attachHandlers();

}


function renderPinGate(returnTab){

  return `

  <div class="card pin-gate">

    <h2>Admin PIN required</h2>

    <p class="muted">This area runs the draw and edits club data. Ask your club admin for the PIN.</p>

    <input type="text" inputmode="numeric" id="pinInput" maxlength="8" placeholder="••••">

    <div class="btn-row" style="justify-content:center">

      <button class="btn-primary" id="pinSubmit" data-return="${returnTab}">Unlock</button>

    </div>

  </div>`;

}


// ---------- REGISTER TAB ----------

function renderRegister(){

  const open = sessionsSorted().filter(isRegistrationOpen);

  const selectedName = state.selectedRegName || "";

  const isGuestSelection = selectedName === "__guest__";

  const anyGuestSessionOpen = open.some(s=>s.guestsAllowed);


  const rosterOpts = state.roster.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name))

    .map(m=>`<option value="${escAttr(m.name)}" ${m.name===selectedName?"selected":""}>${esc(m.name)}${m.junior?" (Jr)":""}</option>`).join("");

  const hostOpts = rosterOpts;


  // Which of the currently open sessions has this shooter already registered for?

  const registeredIds = new Set();

  if(selectedName && !isGuestSelection){

    open.forEach(s => { if(s.registrations.includes(selectedName)) registeredIds.add(s.id); });

  }


  const byWeekend = {};

  open.forEach(s=>{ (byWeekend[s.weekendLabel] = byWeekend[s.weekendLabel] || []).push(s); });


  const groupsHtml = Object.keys(byWeekend).map(wk=>{

    const sess = byWeekend[wk].slice().sort((a,b)=>a.dateISO.localeCompare(b.dateISO));

    const remaining = sess.filter(s=>!registeredIds.has(s.id) && !(isGuestSelection && !s.guestsAllowed));


    if(selectedName && !isGuestSelection && remaining.length===0){

      return `<div class="weekend-group"><div class="session-option locked checked">

        <div class="meta"><div class="sday">${wk}</div><div class="sdate">You're registered for every open session this weekend.</div></div>

        <span class="tag tag-drawn">✓ All set</span>

      </div></div>`;

    }


    const selectAll = remaining.length > 1 ? `

      <label class="session-option select-all" data-weekend="${escAttr(wk)}">

        <input type="checkbox" class="selectAllCheck" data-weekend="${escAttr(wk)}">

        <div class="meta"><div class="sday">Select all ${remaining.length} remaining sessions — ${wk}</div><div class="sdate">Shooting the whole weekend</div></div>

      </label>` : "";


    const rows = sess.map(s=>{

      if(registeredIds.has(s.id)){

        return `<div class="session-option locked checked">

          <div class="meta">

            <div class="sday">${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel}</div>

            <div class="sdate">${fmtDate(s.dateISO)} · Closes ${fmtDateTime(registrationCloseTime(s))}</div>

          </div>

          <span class="tag tag-drawn">✓ Registered</span>

        </div>${rollCallHtml(s)}`;

      }

      if(isGuestSelection && !s.guestsAllowed){

        return `<div class="session-option locked">

          <div class="meta">

            <div class="sday">${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel}</div>

            <div class="sdate">${fmtDate(s.dateISO)} · Guests not allowed for this session</div>

          </div>

        </div>`;

      }

      return `<label class="session-option" data-session="${s.id}" data-weekend="${escAttr(wk)}">

        <input type="checkbox" class="regCheck" value="${s.id}">

        <div class="meta">

          <div class="sday">${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel}</div>

          <div class="sdate">${fmtDate(s.dateISO)} · ${s.registrations.length} registered · Closes ${fmtDateTime(registrationCloseTime(s))}</div>

        </div>

      </label>${rollCallHtml(s)}`;

    }).join("");

    return `<div class="weekend-group">${selectAll}${rows}</div>`;

  }).join("");


  return `

  <div class="card">

    <h2>Who's shooting?</h2>

    <label>Your name</label>

    <select id="regName">

      <option value="">— Select your name —</option>

      ${anyGuestSessionOpen ? `<option value="__guest__" ${isGuestSelection?"selected":""}>Guest / not on roster</option>` : ""}

      ${rosterOpts}

    </select>

    <div id="guestFields" style="display:${isGuestSelection?"block":"none"}">

      <label>Guest name</label>

      <input type="text" id="guestName" placeholder="Full name">

      <label>Hosted by</label>

      <select id="guestHost"><option value="">— Select host —</option>${hostOpts}</select>

    </div>

  </div>


  <div class="card">

    <h2>Open sessions</h2>

    ${open.length===0 ? `<div class="empty">No sessions are open for registration right now.<br>Check back once the club admin opens the next weekend.</div>` : groupsHtml}

    ${open.length>0 ? `<div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-primary" id="submitReg" style="width:100%">Submit registration</button></div>` : ""}

  </div>

  ${propertyMapsCardHtml()}`;

}


function rollCallHtml(s){

  if(!s.registrations || s.registrations.length===0){

    return `<div class="muted" style="padding:2px 2px 8px">No one registered yet.</div>`;

  }

  // Preserve each shooter's original registration-order number (their position

  // in s.registrations, the order they signed up), then list alphabetically

  // for easy lookup during roll call.

  const withNum = s.registrations.map((name,idx)=>({name, num: idx+1}));

  withNum.sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));

  const rows = withNum.map(r=>`<tr><td>${r.num}</td><td>${esc(r.name)}</td></tr>`).join("");

  return `<details class="rollcall">

    <summary>${s.registrations.length} registered — view names for roll call</summary>

    <table><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>Name</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table>

  </details>`;

}

function escAttr(s){ return String(s).replace(/"/g,'&quot;'); }

function esc(s){ return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;'); }


function syncSelectAll(weekend){

  if(!weekend) return;

  const all = document.querySelectorAll(`.session-option[data-session][data-weekend="${cssEsc(weekend)}"] input`);

  const master = document.querySelector(`.selectAllCheck[data-weekend="${cssEsc(weekend)}"]`);

  if(!master || all.length===0) return;

  master.checked = Array.from(all).every(i=>i.checked);

  master.closest(".select-all").classList.toggle("checked", master.checked);

}


async function submitRegistration(){

  const nameSel = document.getElementById("regName").value;

  let name = nameSel;

  if(nameSel === "__guest__"){

    const gname = document.getElementById("guestName").value.trim();

    const host = document.getElementById("guestHost").value;

    if(!gname || !host){ toast("Enter guest name and host to continue."); return; }

    // Guest registration is admin-controlled — a member can fill this in, but

    // it won't submit without the admin PIN, so an admin has to be the one to

    // actually approve and add it.

    const pin = prompt("Admin PIN required to add a guest:");

    if(pin === null) return; // cancelled

    if(pin !== state.adminPin){ toast("Incorrect PIN — guest not added."); return; }

    name = `${gname.toUpperCase()} (Guest of ${host})`;

  } else if(!nameSel){

    toast("Select your name first.");

    return;

  }

  const checks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".regCheck:checked")).map(c=>c.value);

  if(checks.length===0){ toast("Pick at least one session."); return; }


  let added = 0;

  const closedOut = [];

  for(const id of checks){

    // Re-fetch fresh from the shared database right before writing — this catches

    // both the close time passing and an admin manually closing it moments ago,

    // even if this page had already been open with it showing as available.

    const fresh = await sget("session:"+id);

    if(!fresh){ closedOut.push(id); continue; }

    state.sessions[id] = fresh;

    if(!isRegistrationOpen(fresh)){

      closedOut.push(fresh);

      continue;

    }

    if(!fresh.registrations.includes(name)){

      fresh.registrations.push(name);

      await saveSession(fresh);

    }

    added++;

  }


  if(added>0){

    toast(`Registered ${name.split(" (")[0]} for ${added} session${added>1?"s":""}.`);

  }

  if(closedOut.length>0){

    const labels = closedOut.map(s => typeof s === "string" ? "a session" : `${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel}`).join(", ");

    toast(`Registration just closed for: ${labels}. Not added — check with the club admin if this was in error.`, 5000);

  }

  state.selectedRegName = "";

  render();

}


// ---------- DRAW TAB ----------

function renderDraw(){

  const pending = sessionsSorted().filter(s=>!s.locked && (!s.order || !s.order.length) && !s.rollCallLocked && s.registrations.length>0);

  const sessions = sessionsSorted().filter(s=>!s.locked && (s.rollCallLocked || (s.order && s.order.length)));

  const sel = state.drawSessionId && state.sessions[state.drawSessionId] ? state.sessions[state.drawSessionId] : (sessions[0]||null);

  if(sel) state.drawSessionId = sel.id;

  const options = sessions.map(s=>`<option value="${s.id}" ${sel && s.id===sel.id?"selected":""}>${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel} · ${fmtDate(s.dateISO)} (${s.registrations.length})</option>`).join("");


  let body = `<div class="empty">No sessions ready to draw yet. Complete roll call for a session first — it'll show up here once locked.</div>`;

  if(sel){

    const status = sessionStatus(sel);

    const drawn = !!(sel.order && sel.order.length);

    body = `

    <div class="stat-box">

      <div class="s"><div class="n">${sel.registrations.length}</div><div class="l">Registered</div></div>

      <div class="s"><div class="n">${drawn ? Object.keys(sel.assignments||{}).length : "—"}</div><div class="l">Assigned</div></div>

      <div class="s"><div class="n">${status}</div><div class="l">Status</div></div>

      <div class="s"><div class="n" style="font-size:16px">${weatherChipHtml(sel.dateISO)}</div><div class="l">Forecast</div></div>

    </div>

    <div class="muted" style="margin-bottom:4px">Scheduled draw ${fmtDateTime(drawDateTime(sel))} · Registration ${isRegistrationOpen(sel)?"closes":"closed"} ${fmtDateTime(registrationCloseTime(sel))}${sel.forceOpen && isPastCloseTime(sel) ? " · Reopened for late requests" : ""}</div>

    <div class="muted" style="margin-bottom:10px">${sel.availableProperties ? `${sel.availableProperties.length} propert${sel.availableProperties.length===1?"y":"ies"} available this weekend` : "No property list saved for this weekend — showing all active properties"}</div>

    ${propertyMapsRowHtml(sel.availableProperties)}`;


    if(!drawn){

      const earlier = earlierUndrawnSessions(sel);

      if(earlier.length>0){

        body += `<div class="muted" style="margin-bottom:10px;color:var(--oxblood)">⚠ Out of order: ${earlier.map(x=>`${dayLabel(x.day)} · ${x.weekendLabel}`).join(", ")} ${earlier.length>1?"haven't":"hasn't"} been drawn yet. You'll be asked to confirm before proceeding.</div>`;

      }

      body += `<div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-primary" id="runDraw" style="width:100%" ${sel.registrations.length===0?"disabled":""}>Run the draw (${sel.registrations.length} shooters)</button></div>`;

    } else {

      const taken = {}; // property -> Set of stand numbers used in this session

      Object.values(sel.assignments||{}).forEach(a=>{

        if(a && a.property){ (taken[a.property] = taken[a.property]||new Set()).add(String(a.stand)); }

      });

      let sawJunior = false;

      body += sel.order.map((name,i)=>{

        const jr = isJuniorName(name);

        let divider = "";

        if(jr && !sawJunior){ divider = `<div class="section-divider">Junior members draw last</div>`; sawJunior = true; }

        const a = (sel.assignments||{})[name];

        if(a){

          return `${divider}<div class="ticket done">

            <div class="num">${i+1}</div>

            <div class="who"><div class="nm">${esc(name)}${jr?' <span class="tag tag-drawn">Jr</span>':''}</div><div class="as">${esc(a.property)} · Stand ${a.stand}</div></div>

            <button class="btn-ghost btn-small editAssign" data-name="${escAttr(name)}">Edit</button>

          </div>`;

        }

        const availableSet = sel.availableProperties ? new Set(sel.availableProperties) : null;

        const propOpts = state.properties

          .filter(p => p.active && (!availableSet || availableSet.has(p.name)))

          .map(p=>`<option value="${escAttr(p.name)}">${esc(p.name)} (${standList(p).length})</option>`).join("");

        return `${divider}<div class="ticket" data-pending="${escAttr(name)}">

          <div class="num">${i+1}</div>

          <div class="who">

            <div class="nm">${esc(name)}${jr?' <span class="tag tag-drawn">Jr</span>':''}</div>

            <div class="assign-controls">

              <select class="propSel"><option value="">Property…</option>${propOpts}</select>

              <select class="standSel"><option value="">Stand…</option></select>

              <button class="btn-brass btn-small confirmAssign" data-name="${escAttr(name)}">Confirm</button>

            </div>

          </div>

        </div>`;

      }).join("");

      body += `<div class="btn-row">

        <button class="btn-ghost btn-small" id="reshuffle">Re-shuffle order</button>

        <button class="btn-primary" id="lockSession" style="flex:1">Lock session</button>

      </div>`;

      window.__taken = taken;

    }

  }


  return `

  <div class="card">

    <h2>Choose session</h2>

    ${sessions.length ? `<select id="drawSessionSel">${options}</select>` : `<div class="empty">No sessions available.</div>`}

    ${pending.length ? `<div class="muted" style="margin-top:10px">${pending.length} session${pending.length>1?"s":""} waiting on roll call: ${pending.map(s=>`${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel}`).join(", ")}. Lock roll call for ${pending.length>1?"them":"it"} first.</div>` : ""}

  </div>

  <div class="card">${body}</div>`;

}


function shuffle(arr){

  const a = arr.slice();

  for(let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){

    const j = Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));

    [a[i],a[j]] = [a[j],a[i]];

  }

  return a;

}

function earlierUndrawnSessions(s){

  // Chronological order across the whole season (this naturally covers "same

  // weekend" ordering too, since weekend sessions sort within it).

  const all = sessionsSorted();

  const idx = all.findIndex(x=>x.id===s.id);

  if(idx<0) return [];

  return all.slice(0, idx).filter(x =>

    x.registrations.length>0 && (!x.order || x.order.length===0) && !x.locked

  );

}

async function runDraw(){

  const s = state.sessions[state.drawSessionId];

  if(!s || s.registrations.length===0) return;

  const earlier = earlierUndrawnSessions(s);

  if(earlier.length>0){

    const list = earlier.map(x=>`${dayLabel(x.day)} · ${x.weekendLabel} (${fmtDate(x.dateISO)})`).join("\n");

    const ok = confirm(`These earlier sessions haven't been drawn yet:\n\n${list}\n\nDraw ${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel} out of order anyway?`);

    if(!ok) return;

  }

  const ordinary = s.registrations.filter(n => !isJuniorName(n));

  const juniors  = s.registrations.filter(n => isJuniorName(n));

  const order = shuffle(ordinary).concat(shuffle(juniors));

  s.order = order;

  s.assignments = {};

  s.drawnAt = new Date().toISOString();

  await saveSession(s);

  toast("Draw order set.");

  render();

}

async function reshuffleDraw(){

  const s = state.sessions[state.drawSessionId];

  if(!s) return;

  if(Object.keys(s.assignments||{}).length > 0){

    if(!confirm("Some stands are already assigned. Re-shuffling clears all assignments. Continue?")) return;

  }

  await runDraw();

}

async function confirmAssign(name){

  const s = state.sessions[state.drawSessionId];

  const ticket = document.querySelector(`.ticket[data-pending="${cssEsc(name)}"]`);

  if(!ticket) return;

  const prop = ticket.querySelector(".propSel").value;

  const stand = ticket.querySelector(".standSel").value;

  if(!prop || !stand){ toast("Pick both a property and a stand."); return; }

  s.assignments = s.assignments || {};

  s.assignments[name] = {property: prop, stand: stand};

  await saveSession(s);

  render();

}

async function editAssign(name){

  const s = state.sessions[state.drawSessionId];

  delete s.assignments[name];

  await saveSession(s);

  render();

}

async function lockSession(){

  const s = state.sessions[state.drawSessionId];

  const unassigned = s.order.filter(n => !(s.assignments||{})[n]);

  if(unassigned.length>0){

    const list = unassigned.join(", ");

    const ok = confirm(`These ${unassigned.length} hunter(s) don't have a stand assigned yet:\n\n${list}\n\nThis is normal if a stand was released and never re-claimed. Lock the session anyway?`);

    if(!ok) return;

  }

  s.locked = true;

  s.active = false;

  await saveSession(s);

  toast("Session locked. Results are final.");

  state.tab = "results";

  render();

}

function cssEsc(s){ return String(s).replace(/(["\\])/g,'\\$1'); }


// ---------- ROLL CALL TAB ----------

function renderRollCall(){

  const sessions = sessionsSorted().filter(s=>(!s.order || !s.order.length) && !s.locked);

  const sel = state.rollcallSessionId && state.sessions[state.rollcallSessionId] && sessions.some(s=>s.id===state.rollcallSessionId)

    ? state.sessions[state.rollcallSessionId] : (sessions[0]||null);

  if(sel) state.rollcallSessionId = sel.id;

  const options = sessions.map(s=>`<option value="${s.id}" ${sel && s.id===sel.id?"selected":""}>${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel} · ${fmtDate(s.dateISO)} (${s.registrations.length})${s.rollCallLocked?" — locked":""}</option>`).join("");


  if(!sel){

    return `<div class="card"><div class="empty">No sessions ready for roll call.<br>Sessions appear here once they have registrations and haven't been drawn yet.</div></div>`;

  }


  const locked = !!sel.rollCallLocked;

  const registered = sel.registrations.slice();


  const rows = registered.map((name,i)=>{

    const jr = isJuniorName(name);

    return `<div class="ticket">

      <div class="num">${i+1}</div>

      <div class="who">

        <div class="nm">${esc(name)}${jr?' <span class="tag tag-drawn">Jr</span>':''}</div>

      </div>

      ${locked ? "" : `<button class="btn-danger btn-small removeAbsent" data-name="${escAttr(name)}">Not present</button>`}

    </div>`;

  }).join("") || `<div class="empty">No one registered for this session yet.</div>`;


  const rosterOpts = state.roster.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name))

    .filter(m=>!registered.includes(m.name))

    .map(m=>`<option value="${escAttr(m.name)}">${esc(m.name)}${m.junior?" (Jr)":""}</option>`).join("");

  const hostOpts = state.roster.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name))

    .map(m=>`<option value="${escAttr(m.name)}">${esc(m.name)}</option>`).join("");


  const addBlock = locked ? "" : `

    <div class="section-divider">Add a late-comer</div>

    <label>Name</label>

    <select id="rollcallAddName">

      <option value="">— Select name —</option>

      ${sel.guestsAllowed ? `<option value="__guest__">Guest / not on roster</option>` : ""}

      ${rosterOpts}

    </select>

    <div id="rollcallGuestFields" style="display:none">

      <label>Guest name</label>

      <input type="text" id="rollcallGuestName" placeholder="Full name">

      <label>Hosted by</label>

      <select id="rollcallGuestHost"><option value="">— Select host —</option>${hostOpts}</select>

    </div>

    <div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-brass" id="rollcallAdd" style="width:100%">Add to roll call</button></div>`;


  return `

  <div class="card">

    <h2>Choose session</h2>

    ${sessions.length ? `<select id="rollcallSessionSel">${options}</select>` : ""}

    <div class="muted" style="margin:10px 0 4px">

      ${locked

        ? "Roll call locked — this list is what the draw will run against. Unlock to make more changes."

        : "Mark anyone who isn't present as \"Not present,\" and add any late-comers below. Lock roll call when everyone's accounted for — that finalizes the list the draw will use."}

    </div>

    <div class="btn-row">

      <button class="btn-primary" id="toggleRollCallLock" style="width:100%">${locked ? "Unlock roll call" : "Lock roll call"}</button>

    </div>

  </div>

  <div class="card">

    <h2>${registered.length} checked in</h2>

    ${rows}

    ${addBlock}

  </div>`;

}

// Always resolve the session these roll call actions act on from the visible

// dropdown itself, not from separately-tracked state — the two can drift

// apart across re-renders and cause an action to silently fire on the wrong

// session while the screen still shows the one the admin picked.

function currentRollCallSessionId(){

  const sel = document.getElementById("rollcallSessionSel");

  const id = (sel && sel.value) ? sel.value : state.rollcallSessionId;

  if(id) state.rollcallSessionId = id; // keep state in sync for anything else that reads it

  return id;

}

async function markAbsent(name){

  const s = state.sessions[currentRollCallSessionId()];

  if(!s) return;

  if(!confirm(`Remove ${name.split(" (")[0]} as not present? This removes them from this session's registration. This can't be undone.`)) return;

  s.registrations = (s.registrations||[]).filter(n=>n!==name);

  await saveSession(s);

  toast(`Removed ${name.split(" (")[0]}.`);

  render();

}

async function addToRollCall(){

  const s = state.sessions[currentRollCallSessionId()];

  if(!s) return;

  const nameSel = document.getElementById("rollcallAddName").value;

  let name = nameSel;

  if(nameSel === "__guest__"){

    const gname = document.getElementById("rollcallGuestName").value.trim();

    const host = document.getElementById("rollcallGuestHost").value;

    if(!gname || !host){ toast("Enter guest name and host to continue."); return; }

    name = `${gname.toUpperCase()} (Guest of ${host})`;

  } else if(!nameSel){

    toast("Select a name first.");

    return;

  }

  s.registrations = s.registrations || [];

  if(s.registrations.includes(name)){ toast("Already checked in."); return; }

  s.registrations.push(name);

  await saveSession(s);

  toast(`Added ${name.split(" (")[0]} to roll call.`);

  render();

}

async function toggleRollCallLock(){

  const s = state.sessions[currentRollCallSessionId()];

  if(!s) return;

  if(!s.rollCallLocked && s.registrations.length===0){ toast("No one's checked in yet."); return; }

  s.rollCallLocked = !s.rollCallLocked;

  await saveSession(s);

  toast(s.rollCallLocked ? "Roll call locked — ready for the draw." : "Roll call unlocked.");

  render();

}


// ---------- RESULTS TAB ----------

function renderResults(){

  const sessions = sessionsSorted().filter(s=>s.order && s.order.length);

  if(sessions.length===0) return `<div class="card"><div class="empty">No draws have been run yet.</div></div>`;

  const sel = state.resultsSessionId && state.sessions[state.resultsSessionId] ? state.sessions[state.resultsSessionId] : sessions[sessions.length-1];

  state.resultsSessionId = sel.id;

  const options = sessions.map(s=>`<option value="${s.id}" ${s.id===sel.id?"selected":""}>${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel} · ${fmtDate(s.dateISO)}</option>`).join("");

  const bags = sel.bags || {};

  const notShooting = sel.notShooting || {};


  const rows = sel.order.map((name,i)=>{

    const a = (sel.assignments||{})[name];

    const didNotShoot = !!notShooting[name];

    const counts = bags[name];

    const total = bagTotal(counts);

    const breakdown = bagBreakdown(counts);

    const birdsCell = didNotShoot

      ? `<span class="muted">Did not shoot</span>`

      : (total>0 ? `<b>${total}</b>${breakdown?`<div class="muted" style="font-size:11px">${esc(breakdown)}</div>`:""}` : `<span class="muted">—</span>`);

    return `<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${esc(name)}</td><td>${a?esc(a.property):"—"}</td><td>${a?a.stand:"—"}</td>

      <td>${birdsCell}</td></tr>`;

  }).join("");


  const isLocked = sessionStatus(sel)==='locked';

  const selectedBagName = state.selectedBagName || "";

  const nameOpts = sel.order.map(n=>{

    const logged = bags[n] || notShooting[n];

    return `<option value="${escAttr(n)}" ${n===selectedBagName?"selected":""}>${esc(n)}${logged?" ✓":""}</option>`;

  }).join("");

  const speciesInputs = state.species.map(sp=>`

    <div>

      <label>${esc(sp)}</label>

      <input type="text" inputmode="numeric" class="bagInput" data-species="${escAttr(sp)}" placeholder="0">

    </div>`).join("");


  const wasNotShooting = selectedBagName ? !!notShooting[selectedBagName] : false;


  // --- Change/release stand: fully independent card, own name selection,

  // no connection to the did-not-shoot stats checkbox above. ---

  const selectedStandName = state.selectedStandName || "";

  const standNameOpts = sel.order.map(n=>`<option value="${escAttr(n)}" ${n===selectedStandName?"selected":""}>${esc(n)}</option>`).join("");

  const chosenAssign = selectedStandName ? (sel.assignments||{})[selectedStandName] : null;

  const takenByProp = {};

  Object.entries(sel.assignments||{}).forEach(([nm,a])=>{

    if(!a || nm===selectedStandName) return;

    (takenByProp[a.property] = takenByProp[a.property]||new Set()).add(String(a.stand));

  });

  window.__resultsTaken = takenByProp;

  const availableSet = sel.availableProperties ? new Set(sel.availableProperties) : null;

  const propOpts = state.properties

    .filter(p => p.active && (!availableSet || availableSet.has(p.name)))

    .map(p=>`<option value="${escAttr(p.name)}">${esc(p.name)} (${standList(p).length})</option>`).join("");


  const standCard = `

  <div class="card">

    <h2>Change or release a stand</h2>

    <p class="muted">Move to a different available stand, or release a stand back into the pool if that hunter's decided not to shoot. First come, first served — no two hunters can hold the same stand.</p>

    ${propertyMapsRowHtml(sel.availableProperties)}

    <label>Name</label>

    <select id="standName"><option value="">— Select name —</option>${standNameOpts}</select>

    ${selectedStandName ? `

      <p class="muted" style="margin:8px 0">Currently: ${chosenAssign ? `<b>${esc(chosenAssign.property)} · Stand ${chosenAssign.stand}</b>` : "Not assigned"}</p>

      <select id="resultsStandProp"><option value="">Property…</option>${propOpts}</select>

      <select id="resultsStandStand"><option value="">Stand…</option></select>

      <div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-brass btn-small" id="updateStand" style="width:100%">Change to this stand</button></div>

      ${chosenAssign ? `<div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-ghost btn-small" id="releaseStand" style="width:100%">Not shooting — release this stand</button></div>` : ""}

    ` : ""}

  </div>`;


  return `

  ${isLocked ? `

  <div class="card">

    <h2>Log your bag <span class="muted" style="font-weight:400;font-size:13px">· ${sel.weather ? `${weatherIcon(sel.weather.code)} ${sel.weather.tMax}°C on shoot day` : weatherChipHtml(sel.dateISO)}</span></h2>

    <p class="muted">Only shooters who were in this session can log a count. Already-logged names show a ✓ — logging again overwrites your previous entry.</p>

    <label>Your name</label>

    <select id="bagName"><option value="">— Select your name —</option>${nameOpts}</select>

    <label class="session-option" style="margin-top:8px">

      <input type="checkbox" id="didNotShootChk" ${wasNotShooting?"checked":""}>

      <div class="meta"><div class="sday">I did not shoot this session</div><div class="sdate">Excludes you from the season stats entirely</div></div>

    </label>

    <div id="bagCountFields" style="display:${wasNotShooting?"none":"block"}">

      ${state.species.length===0 ? `<p class="muted">No species set up yet — ask the admin to add some under Setup.</p>` : `

        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-top:8px">${speciesInputs}</div>

      `}

    </div>

    <div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-primary" id="submitBag" style="width:100%">Save</button></div>

  </div>` : ""}


  ${standCard}


  <div class="card">

    <h2>Results <span class="muted" style="font-weight:400;font-size:13px">· ${sel.weather ? `${weatherIcon(sel.weather.code)} ${sel.weather.tMax}°C` : weatherChipHtml(sel.dateISO)}</span></h2>

    <select id="resultsSessionSel">${options}</select>

    <div class="muted" style="margin:10px 0 4px">${isLocked?'Final — locked':'Draft — not yet locked'}</div>

    <table>

      <thead><tr><th>#</th><th>Shooter</th><th>Property</th><th>Stand</th><th>Birds</th></tr></thead>

      <tbody>${rows}</tbody>

    </table>

    <div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-ghost" id="exportCsv" style="width:100%">Export CSV</button></div>

  </div>`;

}

async function submitBag(){

  const sel = state.sessions[state.resultsSessionId];

  if(!sel) return;

  const name = document.getElementById("bagName").value;

  if(!name){ toast("Select your name first."); return; }

  const didNotShoot = !!document.getElementById("didNotShootChk")?.checked;

  // Re-fetch fresh so we don't clobber someone else's just-logged count

  const fresh = await sget("session:"+sel.id);

  const target = fresh || sel;

  target.bags = target.bags || {};

  target.notShooting = target.notShooting || {};

  if(didNotShoot){

    delete target.bags[name];

    target.notShooting[name] = true;

    await saveSession(target);

    toast(`Marked ${name.split(" (")[0]} as did not shoot — excluded from stats.`);

  } else {

    delete target.notShooting[name];

    const counts = {};

    document.querySelectorAll(".bagInput").forEach(inp=>{

      const n = parseInt(inp.value, 10);

      if(n && n>0) counts[inp.dataset.species] = n;

    });

    target.bags[name] = counts;

    // Stamp the actual weather for this shoot day, once, the first time anyone logs a bag

    if(!target.weather){

      const w = await fetchWeatherForDate(target.dateISO);

      if(w) target.weather = w;

    }

    await saveSession(target);

    toast(`Saved bag count for ${name.split(" (")[0]}.`);

  }

  state.selectedBagName = "";

  render();

}

async function updateOwnStand(){

  const sel = state.sessions[state.resultsSessionId];

  if(!sel) return;

  const name = document.getElementById("standName").value;

  if(!name){ toast("Select a name first."); return; }

  const propSel = document.getElementById("resultsStandProp");

  const standSel = document.getElementById("resultsStandStand");

  const prop = propSel ? propSel.value : "";

  const stand = standSel ? standSel.value : "";

  if(!prop || !stand){ toast("Pick both a property and a stand."); return; }

  const fresh = await sget("session:"+sel.id);

  const target = fresh || sel;

  target.assignments = target.assignments || {};

  target.assignments[name] = {property: prop, stand: stand};

  await saveSession(target);

  toast(`Updated ${name.split(" (")[0]}'s stand to ${prop} · Stand ${stand}.`);

  state.selectedStandName = name;

  render();

}

async function releaseStand(){

  const sel = state.sessions[state.resultsSessionId];

  if(!sel) return;

  const name = document.getElementById("standName").value;

  if(!name){ toast("Select a name first."); return; }

  const fresh = await sget("session:"+sel.id);

  const target = fresh || sel;

  target.assignments = target.assignments || {};

  if(!target.assignments[name]){ toast("No stand currently assigned to release."); return; }

  if(!confirm(`Release ${name.split(" (")[0]}'s stand back into the pool? Any hunter can grab it after this — first come, first served.`)) return;

  delete target.assignments[name];

  await saveSession(target);

  toast(`Released ${name.split(" (")[0]}'s stand back to the pool.`);

  state.selectedStandName = name;

  render();

}

function exportCsv(){

  const sel = state.sessions[state.resultsSessionId];

  if(!sel) return;

  const bags = sel.bags || {};

  const notShooting = sel.notShooting || {};

  let csv = "Order,Shooter,Property,Stand,Birds,Species Breakdown,Weekend,Day,Date\n";

  sel.order.forEach((name,i)=>{

    const a = (sel.assignments||{})[name] || {};

    const didNotShoot = !!notShooting[name];

    const counts = bags[name];

    const total = didNotShoot ? "Did not shoot" : bagTotal(counts);

    const breakdown = didNotShoot ? "" : bagBreakdown(counts).replace(/"/g,'""');

    csv += `${i+1},"${name.replace(/"/g,'""')}",${a.property||""},${a.stand||""},${total},"${breakdown}",${sel.weekendLabel},${sel.day},${sel.dateISO}\n`;

  });

  const blob = new Blob([csv], {type:"text/csv"});

  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

  const a = document.createElement("a");

  a.href = url; a.download = `${sel.weekendLabel}_${sel.day}_draw.csv`;

  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();

  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

}


// ---------- DASHBOARD TAB ----------

function computeSeasonStats(){

  const lockedSessions = sessionsSorted().filter(s=>s.locked);

  const byProperty = {};

  const bySpecies = {};

  const byShooter = {};

  lockedSessions.forEach(s=>{

    const bags = s.bags || {};

    const assignments = s.assignments || {};

    const notShooting = s.notShooting || {};

    Object.entries(bags).forEach(([name, counts])=>{

      if(notShooting[name]) return; // did not shoot — excluded from stats entirely

      const total = bagTotal(counts);

      if(total<=0) return;

      byShooter[name] = (byShooter[name]||0) + total;

      const propName = assignments[name] ? assignments[name].property : "Unassigned";

      if(!byProperty[propName]) byProperty[propName] = {total:0, species:{}};

      byProperty[propName].total += total;

      Object.entries(counts).forEach(([sp,n])=>{

        n = Number(n)||0;

        if(n<=0) return;

        byProperty[propName].species[sp] = (byProperty[propName].species[sp]||0) + n;

        bySpecies[sp] = (bySpecies[sp]||0) + n;

      });

    });

  });

  const sessionLog = lockedSessions.map(s=>{

    const bags = s.bags || {};

    const notShooting = s.notShooting || {};

    let total = 0;

    Object.entries(bags).forEach(([name,counts]) => { if(!notShooting[name]) total += bagTotal(counts); });

    return {id: s.id, weekendLabel: s.weekendLabel, day: s.day, dateISO: s.dateISO, total, weather: s.weather || null};

  });

  return {lockedSessions, byProperty, bySpecies, byShooter, sessionLog};

}


function renderDashboard(){

  const stats = computeSeasonStats();

  const hasData = Object.keys(stats.byProperty).length > 0;


  const propEntries = Object.entries(stats.byProperty).sort((a,b)=>b[1].total-a[1].total);

  const maxPropTotal = propEntries.length ? propEntries[0][1].total : 0;

  const propBars = propEntries.map(([name,data])=>{

    const pct = maxPropTotal>0 ? Math.round(data.total/maxPropTotal*100) : 0;

    const breakdown = Object.entries(data.species).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).map(([sp,n])=>`${sp} ${n}`).join(' · ');

    return `<div style="margin-bottom:12px">

      <div class="row" style="margin-bottom:4px"><b style="flex:1">${esc(name)}</b><span class="muted" style="flex:0 0 auto">${data.total}</span></div>

      <div style="background:var(--bg);border-radius:6px;overflow:hidden;height:10px;">

        <div style="background:var(--brass);width:${pct}%;height:100%;"></div>

      </div>

      <div class="muted" style="font-size:11px;margin-top:3px">${esc(breakdown)}</div>

    </div>`;

  }).join("");


  const speciesEntries = Object.entries(stats.bySpecies).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);

  const speciesRows = speciesEntries.map(([sp,n])=>`<div class="list-row"><span>${esc(sp)}</span><span><b>${n}</b></span></div>`).join("");


  const shooterEntries = Object.entries(stats.byShooter).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,10);

  const shooterRows = shooterEntries.map(([name,n],i)=>`<div class="list-row"><span>${i+1}. ${esc(name)}</span><span><b>${n}</b></span></div>`).join("");


  const seasonTotal = Object.values(stats.bySpecies).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);


  const lockedSessions = stats.lockedSessions;

  let shootSection = `<div class="empty">No locked shoots yet.</div>`;

  if(lockedSessions.length>0){

    const sel = state.dashboardSessionId && state.sessions[state.dashboardSessionId] && state.sessions[state.dashboardSessionId].locked

      ? state.sessions[state.dashboardSessionId] : lockedSessions[lockedSessions.length-1];

    state.dashboardSessionId = sel.id;

    const options = lockedSessions.map(s=>`<option value="${s.id}" ${s.id===sel.id?"selected":""}>${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel} · ${fmtDate(s.dateISO)}</option>`).join("");


    const byProp = {};

    const bags = sel.bags || {};

    const assignments = sel.assignments || {};

    const notShooting = sel.notShooting || {};

    Object.entries(bags).forEach(([name,counts])=>{

      if(notShooting[name]) return; // did not shoot — excluded from stats entirely

      const total = bagTotal(counts);

      if(total<=0) return;

      const propName = assignments[name] ? assignments[name].property : "Unassigned";

      byProp[propName] = (byProp[propName]||0) + total;

    });

    const shootTotal = Object.values(byProp).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);

    const propList = Object.entries(byProp).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])

      .map(([name,n])=>`<div class="list-row"><span>${esc(name)}</span><span><b>${n}</b></span></div>`).join("");


    shootSection = `

      <select id="dashboardSessionSel">${options}</select>

      <div class="stat-box" style="margin-top:10px"><div class="s"><div class="n">${shootTotal}</div><div class="l">Total birds</div></div></div>

      ${propList || `<div class="empty">No bag counts logged for this shoot yet.</div>`}

    `;

  }


  return `

  <div class="card">

    <h2>This shoot</h2>

    ${shootSection}

  </div>


  <div class="card">

    <h2>Top locations — season to date</h2>

    ${hasData ? `

      <div class="stat-box"><div class="s"><div class="n">${seasonTotal}</div><div class="l">Total birds</div></div><div class="s"><div class="n">${lockedSessions.length}</div><div class="l">Shoots logged</div></div></div>

      ${propBars}

    ` : `<div class="empty">No bag counts logged yet this season.</div>`}

  </div>


  ${speciesEntries.length>0 ? `

  <div class="card">

    <h2>By species — season to date</h2>

    ${speciesRows}

  </div>` : ""}


  ${shooterEntries.length>0 ? `

  <div class="card">

    <h2>Top shooters — season to date</h2>

    ${shooterRows}

  </div>` : ""}


  ${stats.sessionLog.length>0 ? `

  <div class="card">

    <h2>Season log — every weekend</h2>

    <table>

      <thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Session</th><th>Weather</th><th>Birds</th></tr></thead>

      <tbody>${stats.sessionLog.slice().reverse().map(s=>

        `<tr><td>${fmtDate(s.dateISO)}</td><td>${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel}</td><td>${s.weather ? `${weatherIcon(s.weather.code)} ${s.weather.tMax}°C` : `<span class="muted">—</span>`}</td><td><b>${s.total}</b></td></tr>`

      ).join("")}</tbody>

    </table>

    <div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-ghost" id="exportSeasonCsv" style="width:100%">Export full season CSV</button></div>

  </div>` : ""}`;

}

function exportSeasonCsv(){

  const stats = computeSeasonStats();

  let csv = "Weekend,Day,Date,Order,Shooter,Property,Stand,Birds,Species Breakdown\n";

  stats.lockedSessions.forEach(s=>{

    const bags = s.bags || {};

    const assignments = s.assignments || {};

    const notShooting = s.notShooting || {};

    (s.order||[]).forEach((name,i)=>{

      const a = assignments[name] || {};

      const didNotShoot = !!notShooting[name];

      const counts = bags[name];

      const total = didNotShoot ? "Did not shoot" : bagTotal(counts);

      const breakdown = didNotShoot ? "" : bagBreakdown(counts).replace(/"/g,'""');

      csv += `${s.weekendLabel},${s.day},${s.dateISO},${i+1},"${name.replace(/"/g,'""')}",${a.property||""},${a.stand||""},${total},"${breakdown}"\n`;

    });

  });

  const blob = new Blob([csv], {type:"text/csv"});

  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

  const a = document.createElement("a");

  a.href = url; a.download = `WGC_season_bag_log.csv`;

  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();

  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

}


// ---------- SETUP TAB ----------

function renderSetup(){

  const sessions = sessionsSorted();

  const waMsg = buildWeeklyMessage();

  const currentUrl = window.location.href;

  const qrSrc = `https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=260x260&margin=10&data=${encodeURIComponent(currentUrl)}`;

  return `

  <div class="card">

    <h2>Repair tools</h2>

    <p class="muted">If sessions seem to be missing or the list looks out of sync with what's actually on the sheet, this rescans the spreadsheet directly and rebuilds the session list from what's really there. Safe to run any time — it only relinks existing data, it doesn't delete or alter any session's contents.</p>

    <div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-ghost" id="rebuildSessionIndex" style="width:100%">Rebuild session list from sheet</button></div>

    <p class="muted" style="margin-top:14px">Clear out every session — useful for wiping test data before going live. This deletes every session record from the sheet. Registrations, draws, and bag counts for ALL sessions will be gone. Roster, properties, and species are untouched.</p>

    <div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-danger" id="clearAllSessions" style="width:100%">Delete ALL sessions</button></div>

  </div>

  <div class="card" style="text-align:center">

    <h2>Registration QR code</h2>

    <p class="muted">Scan to open the register on your phone. Print it and pin it at the clubhouse, or drop the image straight into WhatsApp.</p>

    <img id="qrImg" src="${qrSrc}" alt="QR code linking to the field register" style="width:220px;height:220px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--line);background:#fff;padding:10px;">

    <div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-ghost" id="downloadQr" style="width:100%">Download QR image</button></div>

  </div>


  <div class="card">

    <h2>This week's WhatsApp message</h2>

    ${waMsg ? `

      <p class="muted">Auto-built from whatever sessions are open right now, with this app's own link. Copy it fresh each week — it always reflects the current status.</p>

      <textarea id="waMsgBox" readonly style="width:100%;min-height:150px;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:12px;padding:10px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;background:#fff;">${esc(waMsg)}</textarea>

      <div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-primary" id="copyWaMsg" style="width:100%">Copy message</button></div>

    ` : `<div class="empty">No sessions are open right now. Create one below, then come back here to copy the announcement.</div>`}

  </div>


  <div class="card">

    <h2>Create a session</h2>

    <label>Weekend label</label>

    <input type="text" id="newWeekendLabel" placeholder="e.g. WK - 7">

    <label>Saturday date</label>

    <input type="date" id="newWeekendDate">

    <label>Sessions to open</label>

    <label class="session-option"><input type="checkbox" id="chkSatAM" checked><div class="meta"><div class="sday">Saturday AM</div></div></label>

    <label class="session-option"><input type="checkbox" id="chkSatPM" checked><div class="meta"><div class="sday">Saturday PM</div></div></label>

    <label class="session-option"><input type="checkbox" id="chkSunAM" checked><div class="meta"><div class="sday">Sunday AM (next day)</div></div></label>

    <label>Properties available this weekend</label>

    <p class="muted">Defaults to every active property. Uncheck any that aren't available this particular weekend.</p>

    <div style="max-height:200px;overflow:auto;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:8px;background:#fff;">

      ${state.properties.filter(p=>p.active).slice().sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name)).map(p=>`

        <label class="session-option" style="margin-bottom:4px;padding:8px;">

          <input type="checkbox" class="newWeekendPropChk" value="${escAttr(p.name)}" checked>

          <div class="meta"><div class="sday">${esc(p.name)} <span class="muted">(${standList(p).length})</span></div></div>

        </label>

      `).join("") || `<div class="empty">No active properties — turn some on above first.</div>`}

    </div>

    <div class="btn-row"><button class="btn-primary" id="createSessions" style="width:100%">Create & open</button></div>

  </div>


  <div class="card">

    <h2>Existing sessions</h2>

    ${sessions.length===0 ? `<div class="empty">None yet.</div>` : sessions.map(s=>`

      <div style="margin-bottom:10px;padding-bottom:10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)">

      <div class="list-row" style="padding:0;border:none">

        <div>

          <div style="font-weight:600">${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel}</div>

          <div class="muted">${fmtDate(s.dateISO)} · <span class="tag tag-${sessionStatus(s)}">${sessionStatus(s)}</span></div>

          <div class="muted">Draw ${fmtDateTime(drawDateTime(s))} · Closes ${fmtDateTime(registrationCloseTime(s))}</div>

          <div class="muted">Guests ${s.guestsAllowed ? '<b style="color:var(--forest)">allowed</b>' : "not allowed"} for this session</div>

        </div>

        <div class="row" style="flex:0 0 auto; gap:6px">

          <button class="btn-small ${s.guestsAllowed ? "btn-brass" : "btn-ghost"} toggleGuestsAllowed" data-id="${s.id}">${s.guestsAllowed ? "Guests: On" : "Guests: Off"}</button>

          ${!s.locked ? (()=>{

            const st = sessionStatus(s);

            const label = st==="open" ? "Close" : st==="auto-closed" ? "Reopen" : "Open";

            const cls = st==="open" ? "btn-ghost" : "btn-brass";

            return `<button class="btn-small ${cls} toggleActive" data-id="${s.id}">${label}</button>`;

          })() : ""}

          <button class="btn-small btn-danger deleteSession" data-id="${s.id}">Delete</button>

        </div>

      </div>

      </div>

    `).join("")}

  </div>


  <div class="card">

    <h2>Roster (${state.roster.length}) · ${state.roster.filter(m=>m.junior).length} junior</h2>

    <p class="muted">Junior members always draw after all ordinary members, even though each group is still randomized within itself.</p>

    <div class="row">

      <input type="text" id="newMemberName" placeholder="Add member name">

      <button class="btn-brass btn-small" id="addMember" style="flex:0 0 auto">Add</button>

    </div>

    <label class="session-option" style="margin-top:8px">

      <input type="checkbox" id="newMemberJunior">

      <div class="meta"><div class="sday">Junior member</div></div>

    </label>

    <div style="max-height:260px;overflow:auto;margin-top:10px">

      ${state.roster.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name)).map(m=>`

        <div class="list-row">

          <span>${esc(m.name)} ${m.junior?'<span class="tag tag-drawn">Jr</span>':''}</span>

          <div class="row" style="flex:0 0 auto; gap:6px">

            <button class="btn-ghost btn-small renameMember" data-name="${escAttr(m.name)}" title="Fix spelling">Rename</button>

            <button class="btn-ghost btn-small toggleJunior" data-name="${escAttr(m.name)}">${m.junior?'Make ordinary':'Make junior'}</button>

            <button class="badge-remove removeMember" data-name="${escAttr(m.name)}">×</button>

          </div>

        </div>

      `).join("")}

    </div>

  </div>


  <div class="card">

    <h2>Properties (${state.properties.length}) · ${state.properties.filter(p=>p.active).length} active</h2>

    <p class="muted">Turned off here means unavailable club-wide until re-enabled. You'll also choose which of the active properties are available for each specific weekend when you create it below.</p>

    <div class="row">

      <input type="text" id="newPropName" placeholder="Property name">

      <input type="text" id="newPropStands" placeholder="# or 1,2,2A…" style="max-width:110px" inputmode="numeric">

      <button class="btn-brass btn-small" id="addProp" style="flex:0 0 auto">Add</button>

    </div>

    <div style="max-height:260px;overflow:auto;margin-top:10px">

      ${state.properties.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name)).map(p=>`

        <div class="list-row">

          <span>${esc(p.name)} <span class="muted">(${standList(p).length} stands)</span> ${p.active?'':'<span class="tag tag-locked">Off</span>'}</span>

          <div class="row" style="flex:0 0 auto; gap:6px">

            <button class="btn-small ${p.active?'btn-ghost':'btn-brass'} togglePropActive" data-name="${escAttr(p.name)}">${p.active?'Turn off':'Turn on'}</button>

            <button class="badge-remove removeProp" data-name="${escAttr(p.name)}">×</button>

          </div>

        </div>

      `).join("")}

    </div>

  </div>


  <div class="card">

    <h2>Species (${state.species.length})</h2>

    <p class="muted">Shown as bag-count entry fields on the Results tab after a session is locked.</p>

    <div class="row">

      <input type="text" id="newSpeciesName" placeholder="Species name">

      <button class="btn-brass btn-small" id="addSpecies" style="flex:0 0 auto">Add</button>

    </div>

    <div style="max-height:220px;overflow:auto;margin-top:10px">

      ${state.species.slice().sort().map(sp=>`

        <div class="list-row"><span>${esc(sp)}</span><button class="badge-remove removeSpecies" data-name="${escAttr(sp)}">×</button></div>

      `).join("")}

    </div>

  </div>


  <div class="card">

    <h2>Admin PIN</h2>

    <div class="row">

      <input type="text" id="newPin" placeholder="New PIN" value="${esc(state.adminPin)}">

      <button class="btn-ghost btn-small" id="savePin" style="flex:0 0 auto">Save</button>

    </div>

    <p class="muted">This PIN is a light gate to keep casual visitors out of Setup and the Draw tools — it is not secure encryption. Anyone with the PIN and the link can edit club data.</p>

  </div>`;

}


async function createSessions(){

  const label = document.getElementById("newWeekendLabel").value.trim();

  const dateStr = document.getElementById("newWeekendDate").value;

  if(!label || !dateStr){ toast("Enter a weekend label and Saturday's date."); return; }

  const wantSatAM = document.getElementById("chkSatAM").checked;

  const wantSatPM = document.getElementById("chkSatPM").checked;

  const wantSunAM = document.getElementById("chkSunAM").checked;

  const picks = [];

  if(wantSatAM) picks.push({key:"SATAM", offset:0});

  if(wantSatPM) picks.push({key:"SATPM", offset:0});

  if(wantSunAM) picks.push({key:"SUNAM", offset:1});

  if(picks.length===0){ toast("Pick at least one session."); return; }


  const availableProperties = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".newWeekendPropChk:checked")).map(c=>c.value);

  if(availableProperties.length===0){ toast("Pick at least one available property for this weekend."); return; }


  for(const p of picks){

    const d = new Date(dateStr+"T12:00:00");

    d.setDate(d.getDate()+p.offset);

    const dISO = d.toISOString().slice(0,10);

    const id = `${label.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi,'').toLowerCase()}-${p.key.toLowerCase()}-${dISO}`;

    const session = {

      id, weekendLabel: label, day: p.key, dateISO: dISO,

      active: true, forceOpen: false, registrations: [], order: [], assignments: {}, bags: {}, drawnAt: null, locked: false, rollCallLocked: false,

      availableProperties, guestsAllowed: false

    };

    await saveSession(session);

    if(!state.sessionIndex.includes(id)) state.sessionIndex.push(id);

  }

  await sset("session-index", state.sessionIndex);

  toast(`Created ${picks.length} session(s) for ${label}.`);

  render();

}

async function toggleActive(id){

  const s = state.sessions[id];

  const status = sessionStatus(s);

  if(status === "open"){

    s.active = false;

    s.forceOpen = false;

    toast(`Closed registration for ${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel}.`);

  } else if(status === "auto-closed"){

    s.forceOpen = true;

    toast(`Reopened ${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel} for late requests.`);

  } else { // "closed"

    s.active = true;

    s.forceOpen = true;

    toast(`Opened registration for ${dayLabel(s.day)} · ${s.weekendLabel}.`);

  }

  await saveSession(s);

  render();

}

async function clearAllSessions(){

  const ids = state.sessionIndex.slice();

  if(ids.length===0){ toast("No sessions to delete."); return; }

  const typed = prompt(`This will permanently delete all ${ids.length} session(s) — every registration, draw, and bag count. Roster/properties/species are untouched.\n\nType DELETE to confirm.`);

  if(typed !== "DELETE"){ toast("Cancelled — nothing deleted."); return; }

  for(const id of ids){

    await apiCall("delete", {key: "session:"+id});

    delete state.sessions[id];

  }

  state.sessionIndex = [];

  await sset("session-index", []);

  toast(`Deleted ${ids.length} session(s). Ready for a fresh season.`);

  render();

}

async function rebuildSessionIndex(){

  if(!confirm("Rescan the spreadsheet for session records and rebuild the list? This won't delete or change any session's data, only reconnect the app to what's actually on the sheet.")) return;

  const r = await apiCall("list", {prefix: "session:"});

  if(!r || r.error || !r.keys){

    toast("⚠️ Couldn't reach the shared database — try again.");

    return;

  }

  const ids = r.keys.map(k=>k.slice("session:".length));

  state.sessionIndex = ids;

  await sset("session-index", ids);

  await loadAllSessions();

  toast(`Found and relinked ${ids.length} session(s). Review the list below and delete any you don't need.`);

  render();

}

async function deleteSession(id){

  if(!confirm("Delete this session and its registrations? This can't be undone.")) return;

  state.sessionIndex = state.sessionIndex.filter(x=>x!==id);

  delete state.sessions[id];

  await sset("session-index", state.sessionIndex);

  await apiCall("delete", {key: "session:"+id}); // actually remove the row, not just unlist it

  render();

}

async function toggleGuestsAllowed(id){

  const s = state.sessions[id];

  if(!s) return;

  s.guestsAllowed = !s.guestsAllowed;

  await saveSession(s);

  toast(s.guestsAllowed ? "Guests allowed for this session." : "Guests turned off for this session.");

  render();

}

async function addMember(){

  const input = document.getElementById("newMemberName");

  const juniorChk = document.getElementById("newMemberJunior");

  const name = input.value.trim().toUpperCase();

  if(!name) return;

  if(!state.roster.find(m=>m.name===name)){

    state.roster.push({name, junior: juniorChk.checked});

    await sset("roster", state.roster);

  }

  input.value = "";

  juniorChk.checked = false;

  render();

}

async function renameMember(oldName){

  const input = window.prompt("Correct spelling for this member:", oldName);

  if(input === null) return; // cancelled

  const newName = input.trim().toUpperCase();

  if(!newName || newName === oldName) return;

  if(state.roster.some(m => m.name === newName)){

    toast(`"${newName}" already exists on the roster — can't rename to a duplicate.`);

    return;

  }

  const member = state.roster.find(m => m.name === oldName);

  if(!member) return;

  member.name = newName;

  await sset("roster", state.roster);


  // Cascade the fix into any session that already references the old spelling

  let touched = 0;

  for(const id of state.sessionIndex){

    const s = state.sessions[id];

    if(!s) continue;

    let changed = false;

    if(s.registrations && s.registrations.includes(oldName)){

      s.registrations = s.registrations.map(n => n===oldName ? newName : n);

      changed = true;

    }

    if(s.order && s.order.includes(oldName)){

      s.order = s.order.map(n => n===oldName ? newName : n);

      changed = true;

    }

    if(s.assignments && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(s.assignments, oldName)){

      s.assignments[newName] = s.assignments[oldName];

      delete s.assignments[oldName];

      changed = true;

    }

    if(changed){

      await sset("session:"+id, s);

      touched++;

    }

  }

  toast(`Renamed "${oldName}" → "${newName}"${touched ? ` and updated ${touched} session${touched>1?"s":""}` : ""}.`);

  render();

}

async function removeMember(name){

  state.roster = state.roster.filter(m=>m.name!==name);

  await sset("roster", state.roster);

  render();

}

async function toggleJunior(name){

  const m = state.roster.find(r=>r.name===name);

  if(!m) return;

  m.junior = !m.junior;

  await sset("roster", state.roster);

  render();

}

async function addProp(){

  const nameInput = document.getElementById("newPropName");

  const standsInput = document.getElementById("newPropStands");

  const name = nameInput.value.trim().toUpperCase();

  const raw = standsInput.value.trim();

  let stands;

  if(/^\d+$/.test(raw)){ stands = parseInt(raw, 10); }

  else { stands = raw.split(",").map(s=>s.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean); }

  const count = Array.isArray(stands) ? stands.length : stands;

  if(!name || !count || count<1){ toast("Enter a property name and its stands — a number (e.g. 8) or a list (e.g. 1,2,2A,3)."); return; }

  const existing = state.properties.find(p=>p.name===name);

  if(existing) existing.stands = stands;

  else state.properties.push({name, stands, active:true});

  await sset("properties", state.properties);

  nameInput.value=""; standsInput.value="";

  render();

}

async function togglePropActive(name){

  const p = state.properties.find(x=>x.name===name);

  if(!p) return;

  p.active = !p.active;

  await sset("properties", state.properties);

  render();

}

async function removeProp(name){

  state.properties = state.properties.filter(p=>p.name!==name);

  await sset("properties", state.properties);

  render();

}

async function addSpecies(){

  const input = document.getElementById("newSpeciesName");

  const name = input.value.trim().toUpperCase();

  if(!name) return;

  if(!state.species.includes(name)){

    state.species.push(name);

    await sset("species", state.species);

  }

  input.value = "";

  render();

}

async function removeSpecies(name){

  state.species = state.species.filter(s=>s!==name);

  await sset("species", state.species);

  render();

}

async function savePin(){

  const val = document.getElementById("newPin").value.trim();

  if(!val){ toast("Enter a PIN."); return; }

  state.adminPin = val;

  await sset("admin-pin", val);

  toast("PIN updated.");

}


function dayLabel(day){

  return day==='SATAM' ? 'Sat AM' : day==='SATPM' ? 'Sat PM' : 'Sun AM';

}

function buildWeeklyMessage(){

  const open = sessionsSorted().filter(s=>s.active && !(s.order&&s.order.length));

  if(open.length===0) return null;

  const byWeekend = {};

  open.forEach(s=>{ (byWeekend[s.weekendLabel] = byWeekend[s.weekendLabel] || []).push(s); });

  const lines = [`Wanderers Gun Club — Draw Registration`, ``];

  Object.keys(byWeekend).forEach(wk=>{

    const sess = byWeekend[wk].slice().sort((a,b)=>a.dateISO.localeCompare(b.dateISO));

    const parts = sess.map(s=>`${dayLabel(s.day)} (${fmtDate(s.dateISO)})`).join(' · ');

    lines.push(`${wk}: ${parts}`);

  });

  lines.push(``, `Tap below and let us know which sessions you're shooting:`, window.location.href, ``, `Registration closes once the draw is run for each session — don't wait!`);

  return lines.join('\n');

}

function fallbackCopy(text){

  const ta = document.getElementById("waMsgBox");

  if(ta){

    ta.focus(); ta.select();

    try{ document.execCommand('copy'); toast("Copied — paste into WhatsApp."); }

    catch(e){ toast("Couldn't auto-copy — tap the text above, select all, and copy manually."); }

  } else {

    toast("Couldn't auto-copy — tap the text above, select all, and copy manually.");

  }

}

function copyText(text){

  if(navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText){

    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(

      ()=>toast("Copied — paste into WhatsApp."),

      ()=>fallbackCopy(text)

    );

  } else {

    fallbackCopy(text);

  }

}


async function downloadQr(){

  const img = document.getElementById('qrImg');

  if(!img) return;

  try{

    const resp = await fetch(img.src, {mode:'cors'});

    const blob = await resp.blob();

    const objUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

    const a = document.createElement('a');

    a.href = objUrl; a.download = 'wanderers-field-register-qr.png';

    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(objUrl);

  }catch(e){

    window.open(img.src, '_blank');

    toast('Opened the QR in a new tab — long-press or right-click to save it.');

  }

}


// ---------- event wiring ----------

function attachHandlers(){

  const regName = document.getElementById("regName");

  if(regName){

    regName.addEventListener("change", ()=>{

      state.selectedRegName = regName.value;

      render();

    });

  }

  document.querySelectorAll(".session-option[data-session]").forEach(opt=>{

    opt.addEventListener("click", (e)=>{

      if(e.target.tagName !== "INPUT"){

        const cb = opt.querySelector("input");

        cb.checked = !cb.checked;

      }

      opt.classList.toggle("checked", opt.querySelector("input").checked);

      syncSelectAll(opt.dataset.weekend);

    });

  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".selectAllCheck").forEach(cb=>{

    cb.addEventListener("click", (e)=> e.stopPropagation());

    cb.addEventListener("change", ()=>{

      const wk = cb.dataset.weekend;

      document.querySelectorAll(`.session-option[data-session][data-weekend="${cssEsc(wk)}"]`).forEach(opt=>{

        const input = opt.querySelector("input");

        input.checked = cb.checked;

        opt.classList.toggle("checked", cb.checked);

      });

    });

  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".select-all[data-weekend]").forEach(opt=>{

    opt.addEventListener("click", (e)=>{

      if(e.target.tagName !== "INPUT"){

        const cb = opt.querySelector("input");

        cb.checked = !cb.checked;

        cb.dispatchEvent(new Event("change"));

      }

      opt.classList.toggle("checked", opt.querySelector("input").checked);

    });

  });

  const submitReg = document.getElementById("submitReg");

  if(submitReg) submitReg.addEventListener("click", submitRegistration);


  const pinSubmit = document.getElementById("pinSubmit");

  if(pinSubmit){

    pinSubmit.addEventListener("click", ()=>{

      const val = document.getElementById("pinInput").value.trim();

      if(val === state.adminPin){ state.adminUnlocked = true; toast("Unlocked."); render(); }

      else toast("Incorrect PIN.");

    });

  }


  const drawSel = document.getElementById("drawSessionSel");

  if(drawSel) drawSel.addEventListener("change", ()=>{ state.drawSessionId = drawSel.value; render(); });

  const runDrawBtn = document.getElementById("runDraw");

  if(runDrawBtn) runDrawBtn.addEventListener("click", runDraw);

  const reshuffleBtn = document.getElementById("reshuffle");

  if(reshuffleBtn) reshuffleBtn.addEventListener("click", reshuffleDraw);

  const lockBtn = document.getElementById("lockSession");

  if(lockBtn) lockBtn.addEventListener("click", lockSession);


  const rollcallSel = document.getElementById("rollcallSessionSel");

  if(rollcallSel) rollcallSel.addEventListener("change", ()=>{ state.rollcallSessionId = rollcallSel.value; render(); });

  document.querySelectorAll(".removeAbsent").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>markAbsent(btn.dataset.name));

  });

  const rollcallAddName = document.getElementById("rollcallAddName");

  if(rollcallAddName){

    rollcallAddName.addEventListener("change", ()=>{

      const gf = document.getElementById("rollcallGuestFields");

      if(gf) gf.style.display = rollcallAddName.value === "__guest__" ? "block" : "none";

    });

  }

  const rollcallAddBtn = document.getElementById("rollcallAdd");

  if(rollcallAddBtn) rollcallAddBtn.addEventListener("click", addToRollCall);

  const toggleLockBtn = document.getElementById("toggleRollCallLock");

  if(toggleLockBtn) toggleLockBtn.addEventListener("click", toggleRollCallLock);


  document.querySelectorAll(".propSel").forEach(sel=>{

    sel.addEventListener("change", ()=>{

      const ticket = sel.closest(".ticket");

      const standSel = ticket.querySelector(".standSel");

      const propName = sel.value;

      const prop = state.properties.find(p=>p.name===propName);

      standSel.innerHTML = `<option value="">Stand…</option>`;

      if(prop){

        const takenSet = (window.__taken && window.__taken[propName]) || new Set();

        standList(prop).forEach(sd=>{

          if(!takenSet.has(sd)) standSel.innerHTML += `<option value="${escAttr(sd)}">${esc(sd)}</option>`;

        });

      }

    });

  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".confirmAssign").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>confirmAssign(btn.dataset.name));

  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".editAssign").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>editAssign(btn.dataset.name));

  });


  const resultsSel = document.getElementById("resultsSessionSel");

  if(resultsSel) resultsSel.addEventListener("change", ()=>{ state.resultsSessionId = resultsSel.value; state.selectedBagName = ""; state.selectedStandName = ""; render(); });

  const exportBtn = document.getElementById("exportCsv");

  if(exportBtn) exportBtn.addEventListener("click", exportCsv);

  const exportSeasonBtn = document.getElementById("exportSeasonCsv");

  if(exportSeasonBtn) exportSeasonBtn.addEventListener("click", exportSeasonCsv);

  const submitBagBtn = document.getElementById("submitBag");

  if(submitBagBtn) submitBagBtn.addEventListener("click", submitBag);


  const bagNameSel = document.getElementById("bagName");

  if(bagNameSel){

    bagNameSel.addEventListener("change", ()=>{

      state.selectedBagName = bagNameSel.value;

      render();

    });

  }

  const didNotShootChk = document.getElementById("didNotShootChk");

  if(didNotShootChk){

    didNotShootChk.addEventListener("change", ()=>{

      const fields = document.getElementById("bagCountFields");

      if(fields) fields.style.display = didNotShootChk.checked ? "none" : "block";

    });

  }

  const standNameSel = document.getElementById("standName");

  if(standNameSel){

    standNameSel.addEventListener("change", ()=>{

      state.selectedStandName = standNameSel.value;

      render();

    });

  }

  const resultsStandProp = document.getElementById("resultsStandProp");

  if(resultsStandProp){

    resultsStandProp.addEventListener("change", ()=>{

      const standSel = document.getElementById("resultsStandStand");

      const propName = resultsStandProp.value;

      const prop = state.properties.find(p=>p.name===propName);

      standSel.innerHTML = `<option value="">Stand…</option>`;

      if(prop){

        const takenSet = (window.__resultsTaken && window.__resultsTaken[propName]) || new Set();

        standList(prop).forEach(sd=>{

          if(!takenSet.has(sd)) standSel.innerHTML += `<option value="${escAttr(sd)}">${esc(sd)}</option>`;

        });

      }

    });

  }

  const updateStandBtn = document.getElementById("updateStand");

  if(updateStandBtn) updateStandBtn.addEventListener("click", updateOwnStand);

  const releaseStandBtn = document.getElementById("releaseStand");

  if(releaseStandBtn) releaseStandBtn.addEventListener("click", releaseStand);


  const dashboardSel = document.getElementById("dashboardSessionSel");

  if(dashboardSel) dashboardSel.addEventListener("change", ()=>{ state.dashboardSessionId = dashboardSel.value; render(); });


  const createBtn = document.getElementById("createSessions");

  if(createBtn) createBtn.addEventListener("click", createSessions);

  document.querySelectorAll(".toggleActive").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>toggleActive(btn.dataset.id));

  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".deleteSession").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>deleteSession(btn.dataset.id));

  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".toggleGuestsAllowed").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>toggleGuestsAllowed(btn.dataset.id));

  });

  const addMemberBtn = document.getElementById("addMember");

  if(addMemberBtn) addMemberBtn.addEventListener("click", addMember);

  document.querySelectorAll(".removeMember").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>removeMember(btn.dataset.name));

  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".renameMember").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>renameMember(btn.dataset.name));

  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".toggleJunior").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>toggleJunior(btn.dataset.name));

  });

  const addPropBtn = document.getElementById("addProp");

  if(addPropBtn) addPropBtn.addEventListener("click", addProp);

  document.querySelectorAll(".removeProp").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>removeProp(btn.dataset.name));

  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".togglePropActive").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>togglePropActive(btn.dataset.name));

  });

  const addSpeciesBtn = document.getElementById("addSpecies");

  if(addSpeciesBtn) addSpeciesBtn.addEventListener("click", addSpecies);

  document.querySelectorAll(".removeSpecies").forEach(btn=>{

    btn.addEventListener("click", ()=>removeSpecies(btn.dataset.name));

  });

  const savePinBtn = document.getElementById("savePin");

  if(savePinBtn) savePinBtn.addEventListener("click", savePin);

  const copyWaBtn = document.getElementById("copyWaMsg");

  if(copyWaBtn) copyWaBtn.addEventListener("click", ()=>{

    const msg = buildWeeklyMessage();

    if(msg) copyText(msg);

  });

  const downloadQrBtn = document.getElementById("downloadQr");

  if(downloadQrBtn) downloadQrBtn.addEventListener("click", downloadQr);

  const rebuildBtn = document.getElementById("rebuildSessionIndex");

  if(rebuildBtn) rebuildBtn.addEventListener("click", rebuildSessionIndex);

  const clearAllBtn = document.getElementById("clearAllSessions");

  if(clearAllBtn) clearAllBtn.addEventListener("click", clearAllSessions);

}


init();
